- [APIListChunking](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/APIListChunking.md): Enable the API clients to retrieve (LIST or GET) resources from API server in chunks. (1 KB)
- [APIPriorityAndFairness](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/APIPriorityAndFairness.md): Enable managing request concurrency with prioritization and fairness at each server. (1 KB)
- [APIResponseCompression](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/APIResponseCompression.md): Compress the API responses for LIST or GET requests. (1 KB)
- [APISelfSubjectReview](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/APISelfSubjectReview.md): Activate the SelfSubjectReview API which allows users to see the requesting subject's authentication information. (1 KB)
- [APIServerIdentity](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/APIServerIdentity.md): Assign each API server an ID in a cluster, using a Lease. (1 KB)

- [APIServingWithRoutine](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/APIServingWithRoutine.md): This feature gate enables an API server performance improvement: the API server can use separate goroutines (lightweight threads managed by the Go runtime) to… (1 KB)

- [AffinityInAnnotations](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/AffinityInAnnotations.md): Enable setting Pod affinity or anti-affinity. (1 KB)
- [AggregatedDiscoveryEndpoint](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/AggregatedDiscoveryEndpoint.md): Enable a single HTTP endpoint /discovery/<version> which supports native HTTP caching with ETags containing all APIResources known to the API server. (1 KB)
- [AllowDNSOnlyNodeCSR](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/AllowDNSOnlyNodeCSR.md): Allow kubelet to request a certificate without any Node IP available, only with DNS names. (1 KB)
- [AllowExtTrafficLocalEndpoints](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/AllowExtTrafficLocalEndpoints.md): Enable a service to route external requests to node local endpoints. (1 KB)

- [AllowParsingUserUIDFromCertAuth](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/AllowParsingUserUIDFromCertAuth.md): When this feature is enabled, the subject name attribute 1.3.6.1.4.1.57683.2 in an X.509 certificate will be parsed as the user UID during certificate authentication. (1 KB)

- [AllowServiceLBStatusOnNonLB](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/AllowServiceLBStatusOnNonLB.md): Enables .status.ingress.loadBalancer to be set on Services of types other than LoadBalancer. (1 KB)

- [CSIPersistentVolume](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CSIPersistentVolume.md): Enable discovering and mounting volumes provisioned through a CSI (Container Storage Interface) compatible volume plugin. (1 KB)
- [CSIServiceAccountToken](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CSIServiceAccountToken.md): Enable CSI drivers to receive the pods' service account token that they mount volumes for. (1 KB)
- [CSIServiceAccountTokenSecrets](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CSIServiceAccountTokenSecrets.md): Enables CSI drivers to opt-in for receiving service account tokens from kubelet through the dedicated secrets field in NodePublishVolumeRequest instead of the volumecontext field. (1 KB)
- [CSIStorageCapacity](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CSIStorageCapacity.md): Enables CSI drivers to publish storage capacity information and the Kubernetes scheduler to use that information when scheduling pods. (1 KB)
- [CSIVolumeFSGroupPolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CSIVolumeFSGroupPolicy.md): Allows CSIDrivers to use the fsGroupPolicy field. (1 KB)
- [CSIVolumeHealth](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CSIVolumeHealth.md): Enable support for CSI volume health monitoring on node. (1 KB)
- [CSRDuration](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CSRDuration.md): Allows clients to request a duration for certificates issued via the Kubernetes CSR API. (1 KB)
- [ChangeContainerStatusOnKubeletRestart](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ChangeContainerStatusOnKubeletRestart.md): Enable legacy writes to update container ready status after the kubelet detects a restart. (1 KB)
- [ClearingNominatedNodeNameAfterBinding](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ClearingNominatedNodeNameAfterBinding.md): Enable clearing .status.nominatedNodeName whenever Pods are bound to nodes. (1 KB)
- [CloudControllerManagerWatchBasedRoutesReconciliation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CloudControllerManagerWatchBasedRoutesReconciliation.md): Enables a watch-based route reconciliation mechanism (rather than reconciling at a fixed interval) within the cloud-controller-manager library. (1 KB)
- [CloudControllerManagerWebhook](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CloudControllerManagerWebhook.md): Enable webhooks in cloud controller manager. (1 KB)
- [CloudDualStackNodeIPs](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CloudDualStackNodeIPs.md): Enables dual-stack kubelet --node-ip with external cloud providers. (1 KB)
- [ClusterTrustBundle](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ClusterTrustBundle.md): This feature gate exists in the Kubernetes API server and the controller manager. (1 KB)
- [ClusterTrustBundleProjection](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ClusterTrustBundleProjection.md): clusterTrustBundle projected volume sources. (1 KB)
- [ComponentFlagz](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ComponentFlagz.md): Enables the component's flagz endpoint. (1 KB)
- [ComponentSLIs](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ComponentSLIs.md): Enable the /metrics/slis endpoint on Kubernetes components like kubelet, kube-scheduler, kube-proxy, kube-controller-manager, cloud-controller-manager allowing you to scrape health check metrics. (1 KB)
- [ComponentStatusz](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ComponentStatusz.md): Enables the component's statusz endpoint. (1 KB)
- [CompositePodGroup](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CompositePodGroup.md): Enable hierarchical gang scheduling for CompositePodGroups and PodGroups. (1 KB)
- [ConcurrentWatchObjectDecode](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ConcurrentWatchObjectDecode.md): Enable concurrent watch object decoding. (1 KB)
- [ConfigurableFSGroupPolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ConfigurableFSGroupPolicy.md): Allows user to configure volume permission change policy for fsGroups when mounting a volume in a Pod. (1 KB)
- [ConsistentHTTPGetHandlers](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ConsistentHTTPGetHandlers.md): Normalize HTTP get URL and Header passing for lifecycle handlers with probers. (1 KB)
- [ConsistentListFromCache](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ConsistentListFromCache.md): Enhance Kubernetes API server performance by serving consistent list requests directly from its watch cache, improving scalability and response times. (1 KB)
- [ConstrainedImpersonation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ConstrainedImpersonation.md): Enables impersonation that is constrained to specific requests instead of being all or nothing. (1 KB)
- [ContainerCheckpoint](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ContainerCheckpoint.md): Enables the kubelet checkpoint API. (1 KB)
- [ContainerRestartRules](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ContainerRestartRules.md): Enables the ability to configure container-level restart policy and restart rules. (1 KB)
- [ContainerStopSignals](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ContainerStopSignals.md): Enables usage of the StopSignal lifecycle for containers for configuring custom stop signals using which the containers would be stopped. (1 KB)
- [ContextualLogging](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ContextualLogging.md): Enables extra details in log output of Kubernetes components that support contextual logging. (1 KB)
- [ControllerManagerLeaderMigration](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ControllerManagerLeaderMigration.md): Enables Leader Migration for kube-controller-manager and cloud-controller-manager which allows a cluster operator to live migrate controllers from the kube-controller-manager into an external controller-manager (e.g. (1 KB)
- [ControllerManagerReleaseLeaderElectionLockOnExit](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ControllerManagerReleaseLeaderElectionLockOnExit.md): Enables the kube-controller-manager to actively release its leader election lock during leader transitions, rather than waiting for the lock's TTL to expire. (1 KB)
- [CoordinatedLeaderElection](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CoordinatedLeaderElection.md): Enables the behaviors supporting the LeaseCandidate API, and also enables coordinated leader election for the Kubernetes control plane, deterministically. (1 KB)
- [CronJobControllerV2](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CronJobControllerV2.md): Use an alternative implementation of the CronJob controller. (1 KB)
- [CronJobTimeZone](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CronJobTimeZone.md): Allow the use of the timeZone optional field in CronJobs (1 KB)
- [CronJobsScheduledAnnotation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CronJobsScheduledAnnotation.md): Set the scheduled job time as an annotation on Jobs that were created on behalf of a CronJob. (1 KB)
- [CrossNamespaceVolumeDataSource](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CrossNamespaceVolumeDataSource.md): Enable the usage of cross namespace volume data source to allow you to specify a source namespace in the dataSourceRef field of a PersistentVolumeClaim. (1 KB)
- [CustomCPUCFSQuotaPeriod](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CustomCPUCFSQuotaPeriod.md): Enable nodes to change cpuCFSQuotaPeriod in kubelet config. (1 KB)
- [CustomPodDNS](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CustomPodDNS.md): Enable customizing the DNS settings for a Pod using its dnsConfig property. (1 KB)
- [CustomResourceDefaulting](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CustomResourceDefaulting.md): Enable CRD support for default values in OpenAPI v3 validation schemas. (1 KB)
- [CustomResourceFieldSelectors](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CustomResourceFieldSelectors.md): Enable selectableFields in the CustomResourceDefinition API to allow filtering of custom resource list, watch and deletecollection requests. (1 KB)
- [CustomResourcePublishOpenAPI](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CustomResourcePublishOpenAPI.md): Enables publishing of CRD OpenAPI specs. (1 KB)
- [CustomResourceSubresources](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CustomResourceSubresources.md): Enable /status and /scale subresources on resources created from CustomResourceDefinition. (1 KB)
- [CustomResourceValidation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CustomResourceValidation.md): Enable schema based validation on resources created from CustomResourceDefinition. (1 KB)
- [CustomResourceValidationExpressions](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CustomResourceValidationExpressions.md): Enable expression language validation in CRD which will validate customer resource based on validation rules written in the x-kubernetes-validations extension. (1 KB)
- [CustomResourceWebhookConversion](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/CustomResourceWebhookConversion.md): Enable webhook-based conversion on resources created from CustomResourceDefinition. (1 KB)
- [DRAAdminAccess](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAAdminAccess.md): Enables support for requesting admin access in a ResourceClaim or a ResourceClaimTemplate. (1 KB)
- [DRAConsumableCapacity](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAConsumableCapacity.md): Enables device sharing across multiple ResourceClaims or requests. (1 KB)
- [DRAControlPlaneController](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAControlPlaneController.md): Enables support for resources with custom parameters and a lifecycle that is independent of a Pod. (1 KB)
- [DRADerivedAttributes](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRADerivedAttributes.md): Enables derivedAttributes in Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA), letting ResourceClaim and ResourceClaimTemplate authors compute virtual device attributes with per-device CEL expressions, for use in matchAttribute… (1 KB)
- [DRADeviceBindingConditions](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRADeviceBindingConditions.md): Enables support for DeviceBindingConditions in the DRA related fields. (1 KB)
- [DRADeviceCompatibilityGroups](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRADeviceCompatibilityGroups.md): Enables support for device compatibility groups in DRA. (1 KB)
- [DRADeviceTaintRules](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRADeviceTaintRules.md): Enables support for tainting devices through DeviceTaintRule objects when using dynamic resource allocation to manage devices. (1 KB)
- [DRADeviceTaints](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRADeviceTaints.md): Enables support for tainting devices and selectively tolerating those taints when using dynamic resource allocation to manage devices. (1 KB)
- [DRAExtendedResource](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAExtendedResource.md): Enables support for the Extended Resource allocation by DRA feature. (1 KB)
- [DRAListTypeAttributes](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAListTypeAttributes.md): Enables list-type attribute fields (bools, ints, strings, versions) for devices in ResourceSlice, allowing a device to advertise multiple values for a single attribute. (1 KB)
- [DRANodeAllocatableResources](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRANodeAllocatableResources.md): Enables the kube-scheduler to incorporate node allocatable resources (such as CPU, memory, and hugepages) managed by Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA) into its standard node… (2 KB)
- [DRAOptionalNodeOperations](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAOptionalNodeOperations.md): Enables support for optional node-local operations in Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA). (1 KB)
- [DRAPartitionableDevices](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAPartitionableDevices.md): Enables support for requesting Partitionable Devices for DRA. (1 KB)
- [DRAPartitionableDevicesType](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAPartitionableDevicesType.md): Enables the PartitionTypeAttribute field on ResourceSlices, which opts a partitionable resource pool into the typed partition summary view of ResourcePoolStatusRequest. (1 KB)
- [DRAPrioritizedList](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAPrioritizedList.md): Allows specifying a prioritized list of alternative devices that can be allocated to a request in a claim if the preferred alternative is not… (1 KB)
- [DRAResourceClaimDeviceStatus](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAResourceClaimDeviceStatus.md): Enables support the ResourceClaim.status.devices field and for setting this status from DRA drivers. (1 KB)
- [DRAResourceClaimGranularStatusAuthorization](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAResourceClaimGranularStatusAuthorization.md): Enables support for granular authorization of ResourceClaim status updates. (1 KB)
- [DRAResourcePoolStatus](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAResourcePoolStatus.md): Enables the ResourcePoolStatusRequest API for querying the availability of devices in DRA resource pools. (1 KB)
- [DRASchedulerFilterTimeout](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRASchedulerFilterTimeout.md): Enables aborting the per-node filter operation in the scheduler after a certain time (10 seconds by default, configurable in the DynamicResources scheduler plugin configuration). (1 KB)
- [DRAWorkloadResourceClaims](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DRAWorkloadResourceClaims.md): Enables PodGroup resources from the Workload API to make requests for devices through Dynamic Resource Allocation that can be shared by their member Pods. (1 KB)
- [DaemonSetUpdateSurge](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DaemonSetUpdateSurge.md): Enables the DaemonSet workloads to maintain availability during update per node. (1 KB)
- [DeclarativeValidation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DeclarativeValidation.md): Reports differences between declarative validation of in-tree Kubernetes APIs and the equivalent hand-written validation. (1 KB)
- [DeclarativeValidationBeta](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DeclarativeValidationBeta.md): The global safety switch for Beta validation rules (+k8s:beta). (1 KB)
- [DeclarativeValidationTakeover](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DeclarativeValidationTakeover.md): Deprecated: replaced by the DeclarativeValidationBeta feature gate. (1 KB)
- [DefaultHostNetworkHostPortsInPodTemplates](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DefaultHostNetworkHostPortsInPodTemplates.md): This feature gate controls the point at which a default value for .spec.containers[].ports[].hostPort is assigned, for Pods using hostNetwork: true. (1 KB)
- [DefaultPodSysctls](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DefaultPodSysctls.md): Enables the defaultPodSysctls field in KubeletConfiguration, allowing Node administrators to specify a default set of namespaced kernel parameters (sysctls) that the kubelet applies to… (1 KB)
- [DefaultPodTopologySpread](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DefaultPodTopologySpread.md): Enables the use of PodTopologySpread scheduling plugin to do default spreading. (1 KB)
- [DelegateFSGroupToCSIDriver](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DelegateFSGroupToCSIDriver.md): If supported by the CSI driver, delegates the role of applying fsGroup from a Pod's securityContext to the driver by passing fsGroup through the… (1 KB)
- [DeploymentReplicaSetTerminatingReplicas](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DeploymentReplicaSetTerminatingReplicas.md): Enables a new status field .status.terminatingReplicas in Deployments and ReplicaSets to allow tracking of terminating pods. (1 KB)
- [DetectCacheInconsistency](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DetectCacheInconsistency.md): Enable cache inconsistency detection in the API server. (1 KB)
- [DevicePluginCDIDevices](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DevicePluginCDIDevices.md): Enable support to CDI device IDs in the Device Plugin API. (1 KB)
- [DevicePlugins](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DevicePlugins.md): Enable the device-plugins based resource provisioning on nodes. (1 KB)
- [DisableAcceleratorUsageMetrics](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DisableAcceleratorUsageMetrics.md): Disable accelerator metrics collected by the kubelet. (1 KB)
- [DisableAllocatorDualWrite](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DisableAllocatorDualWrite.md): You can enable the MultiCIDRServiceAllocator feature gate. (1 KB)
- [DisableCPUQuotaWithExclusiveCPUs](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DisableCPUQuotaWithExclusiveCPUs.md): When the feature gate DisableCPUQuotaWithExclusiveCPUs is enabled (the default), then Kubernetes does not enforce CPU quota for Pods that use the Guaranteed QoS class. (1 KB)
- [DisableCloudProviders](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DisableCloudProviders.md): Enabling this feature gate deactivated functionality in kube-apiserver, kube-controller-manager and kubelet that related to the --cloud-provider command line argument. (1 KB)
- [DisableKubeletCloudCredentialProviders](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DisableKubeletCloudCredentialProviders.md): Enabling the feature gate deactivated the legacy in-tree functionality within the kubelet, that allowed the kubelet to authenticate to a cloud provider container registry… (1 KB)
- [DisableNodeKubeProxyVersion](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DisableNodeKubeProxyVersion.md): Disable setting the kubeProxyVersion field of the Node. (1 KB)
- [DownwardAPIHugePages](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DownwardAPIHugePages.md): Enables usage of hugepages in downward API. (1 KB)
- [DryRun](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DryRun.md): Enable server-side dry run requests so that validation, merging, and mutation can be tested without committing. (1 KB)
- [DynamicAuditing](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DynamicAuditing.md): Used to enable dynamic auditing before v1.19. (1 KB)
- [DynamicKubeletConfig](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DynamicKubeletConfig.md): Enable the dynamic configuration of kubelet. (1 KB)
- [DynamicProvisioningScheduling](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DynamicProvisioningScheduling.md): Extend the default scheduler to be aware of volume topology and handle PV provisioning. (1 KB)
- [DynamicResourceAllocation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DynamicResourceAllocation.md): Enables support for resources with custom parameters and a lifecycle that is independent of a Pod. (1 KB)
- [DynamicVolumeProvisioning](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/DynamicVolumeProvisioning.md): Enable the dynamic provisioning of persistent volumes to Pods. (1 KB)
- [EfficientWatchResumption](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EfficientWatchResumption.md): Allows for storage-originated bookmark (progress notify) events to be delivered to the users. (1 KB)
- [ElasticIndexedJob](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ElasticIndexedJob.md): Enables Indexed Jobs to be scaled up or down by mutating both spec.completions and spec.parallelism together such that spec.completions == spec.parallelism. (1 KB)
- [EmptyDirVolumeMode](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EmptyDirVolumeMode.md): Enables setting Unix permission bits on emptyDir volume directories using the mode field in emptyDir volume sources. (1 KB)
- [EnableAggregatedDiscoveryTimeout](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EnableAggregatedDiscoveryTimeout.md): Enable the five second timeout on aggregated discovery calls. (1 KB)
- [EnableEquivalenceClassCache](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EnableEquivalenceClassCache.md): Enable the scheduler to cache equivalence of nodes when scheduling Pods. (1 KB)
- [EndpointSlice](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EndpointSlice.md): Enables EndpointSlices for more scalable and extensible network endpoints. (1 KB)
- [EndpointSliceNodeName](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EndpointSliceNodeName.md): Enables EndpointSlice nodeName field. (1 KB)
- [EndpointSliceProxying](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EndpointSliceProxying.md): When enabled, kube-proxy running on Linux will use EndpointSlices as the primary data source instead of Endpoints, enabling scalability and performance improvements. (1 KB)
- [EndpointSliceTerminatingCondition](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EndpointSliceTerminatingCondition.md): Enables EndpointSlice terminating and serving condition fields. (1 KB)
- [EnvFiles](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EnvFiles.md): Support defining container's Environment Variable Values via File. (1 KB)
- [EphemeralContainers](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EphemeralContainers.md): Enable the ability to add ephemeral containers to running Pods. (1 KB)
- [EtcdRangeStream](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EtcdRangeStream.md): Enables the kube-apiserver to use etcd's RangeStream RPC to stream large list responses from etcd, instead of fetching them in a single Range response. (1 KB)
- [EvenPodsSpread](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EvenPodsSpread.md): Enable pods to be scheduled evenly across topology domains. (1 KB)
- [EventedPLEG](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/EventedPLEG.md): Enable support for the kubelet to receive container life cycle events from the container runtime via an extension to CRI. (1 KB)
- [ExcludeAdmissionWebhookVirtualResources](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ExcludeAdmissionWebhookVirtualResources.md): Exclude non-persisted (virtual) authentication and authorization resources, such as TokenReview and SubjectAccessReview, from admission webhooks. (1 KB)
- [ExecProbeTimeout](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ExecProbeTimeout.md): Ensure kubelet respects exec probe timeouts. (1 KB)
- [ExpandCSIVolumes](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ExpandCSIVolumes.md): Enable the expanding of CSI volumes. (1 KB)
- [ExpandInUsePersistentVolumes](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ExpandInUsePersistentVolumes.md): Enable expanding in-use PVCs. (1 KB)
- [ExpandPersistentVolumes](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ExpandPersistentVolumes.md): Enable the expanding of persistent volumes. (1 KB)
- [ExpandedDNSConfig](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ExpandedDNSConfig.md): Enable kubelet and kube-apiserver to allow more DNS search paths and longer list of DNS search paths. (1 KB)
- [ExperimentalCriticalPodAnnotation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ExperimentalCriticalPodAnnotation.md): Enable annotating specific pods as critical so that their scheduling is guaranteed. (1 KB)
- [ExperimentalHostUserNamespaceDefaulting](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ExperimentalHostUserNamespaceDefaulting.md): Enabling the defaulting user namespace to host. (1 KB)
- [ExternalPolicyForExternalIP](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ExternalPolicyForExternalIP.md): Fix a bug where ExternalTrafficPolicy is not applied to Service ExternalIPs. (1 KB)
- [ExternalServiceAccountTokenSigner](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ExternalServiceAccountTokenSigner.md): Enable setting --service-account-signing-endpoint to make the kube-apiserver use external signer for token <example-value> and token <example-value> key management. (1 KB)
- [GCERegionalPersistentDisk](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/GCERegionalPersistentDisk.md): Enable the regional PD feature on GCE. (1 KB)
- [GRPCContainerProbe](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/GRPCContainerProbe.md): Enables the gRPC probe method for {Liveness,Readiness,Startup}Probe. (1 KB)
- [GRPCContainerProbeTLS](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/GRPCContainerProbeTLS.md): Enables TLS support for gRPC container probes. (1 KB)
- [GangScheduling](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/GangScheduling.md): Enables the GangScheduling plugin in kube-scheduler, which implements "all-or-nothing" scheduling algorithm. (1 KB)
- [GenericEphemeralVolume](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/GenericEphemeralVolume.md): Enables ephemeral, inline volumes that support all features of normal volumes (can be provided by third-party storage vendors, storage capacity tracking, restore from snapshot,… (1 KB)
- [GenericWorkload](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/GenericWorkload.md): Enables support for the Workload API and PodGroup API to express scheduling requirements at the workload level. (1 KB)
- [GitRepoVolumeDriver](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/GitRepoVolumeDriver.md): This controls if the gitRepo volume plugin is supported or not. (1 KB)
- [GracefulNodeShutdown](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/GracefulNodeShutdown.md): Enables support for graceful shutdown in kubelet. (1 KB)
- [GracefulNodeShutdownBasedOnPodPriority](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/GracefulNodeShutdownBasedOnPodPriority.md): Enables the kubelet to check Pod priorities when shutting down a node gracefully. (1 KB)
- [H2CContainerProbe](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/H2CContainerProbe.md): Enables HTTP/2 cleartext (h2c) support for HTTP container probes. (1 KB)
- [HPAConfigurableTolerance](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/HPAConfigurableTolerance.md): Enables setting a tolerance threshold for HorizontalPodAutoscaler metrics. (1 KB)
- [HPAContainerMetrics](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/HPAContainerMetrics.md): Allow HorizontalPodAutoscalers to scale based on metrics from individual containers within target pods. (1 KB)
- [HPAScaleToZero](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/HPAScaleToZero.md): Enables setting minReplicas to 0 for HorizontalPodAutoscaler resources when using custom or external metrics. (1 KB)
- [HonorPVReclaimPolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/HonorPVReclaimPolicy.md): Honor persistent volume reclaim policy when it is Delete irrespective of PV-PVC deletion ordering. (1 KB)
- [HostnameOverride](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/HostnameOverride.md): Allows setting any FQDN as the pod's hostname. (1 KB)
- [HugePageStorageMediumSize](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/HugePageStorageMediumSize.md): Enable support for multiple sizes pre-allocated huge pages. (1 KB)
- [HugePages](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/HugePages.md): Enable the allocation and consumption of pre-allocated huge pages. (1 KB)
- [HugepageAwareEviction](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/HugepageAwareEviction.md): Subtracts hugepage capacity from memory.available so the kubelet's eviction signal reflects actual regular-memory availability. (1 KB)
- [HyperVContainer](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/HyperVContainer.md): Enable Hyper-V isolation for Windows containers. (1 KB)
- [IPTablesOwnershipCleanup](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/IPTablesOwnershipCleanup.md): This causes kubelet to no longer create legacy iptables rules. (1 KB)
- [IPv6DualStack](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/IPv6DualStack.md): Enable dual stack support for IPv6. (1 KB)
- [IdentifyPodOS](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/IdentifyPodOS.md): Allows the Pod OS field to be specified. (1 KB)
- [ImageMaximumGCAge](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ImageMaximumGCAge.md): Enables the kubelet configuration field imageMaximumGCAge, allowing an administrator to specify the age after which an image will be garbage collected. (1 KB)
- [ImageVolume](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ImageVolume.md): Allow using the image volume source in a Pod. (1 KB)
- [ImageVolumeWithDigest](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ImageVolumeWithDigest.md): For each image volume in a Pod, image digest as part of the pod's status. (1 KB)
- [ImmutableEphemeralVolumes](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ImmutableEphemeralVolumes.md): Allows for marking individual Secrets and ConfigMaps as immutable for better safety and performance. (1 KB)
- [InOrderInformers](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InOrderInformers.md): Force the informers to deliver watch stream events in order instead of out of order. (1 KB)
- [InPlacePodLevelResourcesVerticalScaling](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InPlacePodLevelResourcesVerticalScaling.md): Enables the in-place vertical scaling of resources for a Pod (For example, changing a running Pod's pod-level CPU or memory requests/limits without needing to… (1 KB)
- [InPlacePodVerticalScaling](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InPlacePodVerticalScaling.md): Enables in-place Pod vertical scaling. (1 KB)
- [InPlacePodVerticalScalingAllocatedStatus](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InPlacePodVerticalScalingAllocatedStatus.md): Enables the allocatedResources field in the container status. (1 KB)
- [InPlacePodVerticalScalingExclusiveCPUs](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InPlacePodVerticalScalingExclusiveCPUs.md): Enable resource resizing for containers in Guaranteed pods with integer CPU requests. (1 KB)
- [InPlacePodVerticalScalingExclusiveMemory](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InPlacePodVerticalScalingExclusiveMemory.md): Allow resource resize for containers in Guaranteed Pods when the memory manager policy is set to "Static". (1 KB)
- [InPlacePodVerticalScalingMemoryBackedVolumes](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InPlacePodVerticalScalingMemoryBackedVolumes.md): Enables in-place vertical scaling for memory-backed emptyDir volume size limits. (1 KB)
- [InPlacePodVerticalScalingSchedulerPreemption](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InPlacePodVerticalScalingSchedulerPreemption.md): Enables scheduler preemption of lower-priority pods to fulfill deferred in-place pod resize requests. (1 KB)
- [InTreePluginAWSUnregister](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InTreePluginAWSUnregister.md): Stops registering the aws-ebs in-tree plugin in kubelet and volume controllers. (1 KB)
- [InTreePluginAzureDiskUnregister](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InTreePluginAzureDiskUnregister.md): Stops registering the azuredisk in-tree plugin in kubelet and volume controllers. (1 KB)
- [InTreePluginAzureFileUnregister](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InTreePluginAzureFileUnregister.md): Stops registering the azurefile in-tree plugin in kubelet and volume controllers. (1 KB)
- [InTreePluginGCEUnregister](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InTreePluginGCEUnregister.md): Stops registering the gce-pd in-tree plugin in kubelet and volume controllers. (1 KB)
- [InTreePluginOpenStackUnregister](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InTreePluginOpenStackUnregister.md): Stops registering the OpenStack cinder in-tree plugin in kubelet and volume controllers. (1 KB)
- [InTreePluginPortworxUnregister](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InTreePluginPortworxUnregister.md): Stops registering the Portworx in-tree plugin in kubelet and volume controllers. (1 KB)
- [InTreePluginRBDUnregister](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InTreePluginRBDUnregister.md): Stops registering the RBD in-tree plugin within kubelet and volume controllers. (1 KB)
- [InTreePluginvSphereUnregister](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InTreePluginvSphereUnregister.md): Stops registering the vSphere in-tree plugin in kubelet and volume controllers. (1 KB)
- [IndexedJob](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/IndexedJob.md): Allows the Job controller to manage Pod completions per completion index. (1 KB)
- [InformerResourceVersion](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/InformerResourceVersion.md): Allow clients to use the LastSyncResourceVersion() call on informers, enabling them to perform actions based on the current resource version. (1 KB)
- [IngressClassNamespacedParams](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/IngressClassNamespacedParams.md): Allow namespace-scoped parameters reference in IngressClass resource. (1 KB)
- [Initializers](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/Initializers.md): Allow asynchronous coordination of object creation using the Initializers admission plugin. (1 KB)
- [JobBackoffLimitPerIndex](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/JobBackoffLimitPerIndex.md): Allows specifying the maximal number of pod retries per index in Indexed jobs. (1 KB)
- [JobManagedBy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/JobManagedBy.md): Allows to delegate reconciliation of a Job object to an external controller. (1 KB)
- [JobMutableNodeSchedulingDirectives](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/JobMutableNodeSchedulingDirectives.md): Allows updating node scheduling directives in the pod template of Job. (1 KB)
- [JobPodFailurePolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/JobPodFailurePolicy.md): Allow users to specify handling of pod failures based on container exit codes and pod conditions. (1 KB)
- [JobPodReplacementPolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/JobPodReplacementPolicy.md): Allows you to specify pod replacement for terminating pods in a Job (1 KB)
- [JobReadyPods](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/JobReadyPods.md): Enables tracking the number of Pods that have a Ready condition. (1 KB)
- [JobSuccessPolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/JobSuccessPolicy.md): Allow users to specify when a Job can be declared as succeeded based on the set of succeeded pods. (1 KB)
- [JobTrackingWithFinalizers](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/JobTrackingWithFinalizers.md): Enables tracking Job completions without relying on Pods remaining in the cluster indefinitely. (1 KB)
- [KMSv1](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KMSv1.md): Enables KMS v1 API for encryption at rest. (1 KB)
- [KMSv2](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KMSv2.md): Enables KMS v2 API for encryption at rest. (1 KB)
- [KMSv2KDF](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KMSv2KDF.md): Enables KMS v2 to generate single use data encryption keys. (1 KB)
- [KubeProxyDrainingTerminatingNodes](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeProxyDrainingTerminatingNodes.md): Implement connection draining for terminating nodes for externalTrafficPolicy: Cluster services. (1 KB)
- [KubeProxyIPVS](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeProxyIPVS.md): Enable support for the deprecated ipvs proxy mode in kube-proxy. (1 KB)
- [KubeProxyNFTablesLocalhostNodePorts](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeProxyNFTablesLocalhostNodePorts.md): Enables localhost NodePort Service proxying with the nftables mode of kube-proxy. (1 KB)
- [KubeletCgroupDriverFromCRI](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletCgroupDriverFromCRI.md): Enable detection of the kubelet cgroup driver configuration option from the CRI. (1 KB)
- [KubeletConfigFile](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletConfigFile.md): Enable loading kubelet configuration from a file specified using a config file. (1 KB)
- [KubeletCrashLoopBackOffMax](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletCrashLoopBackOffMax.md): Enables support for configurable per-node backoff maximums for restarting containers in the CrashLoopBackOff state. (1 KB)
- [KubeletCredentialProviders](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletCredentialProviders.md): Enable kubelet exec credential <example-value> for image pull credentials. (1 KB)
- [KubeletEnsureSecretPulledImages](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletEnsureSecretPulledImages.md): Ensure that pods requesting an image are authorized to access the image with the provided credentials when the image is already present on the… (1 KB)
- [KubeletFineGrainedAuthz](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletFineGrainedAuthz.md): Enable fine-grained authorization for the kubelet's HTTP(s) API. (1 KB)
- [KubeletInUserNamespace](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletInUserNamespace.md): Enables support for running kubelet in a user namespace. (1 KB)
- [KubeletPSI](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletPSI.md): Enable kubelet to surface Pressure Stall Information (PSI) metrics in the Summary API and Prometheus metrics. (1 KB)
- [KubeletPluginsWatcher](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletPluginsWatcher.md): Enable probe-based plugin watcher utility to enable kubelet to discover plugins such as CSI volume drivers. (1 KB)
- [KubeletPodResources](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletPodResources.md): Enable the kubelet's pod resources gRPC endpoint. (1 KB)
- [KubeletPodResourcesDynamicResources](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletPodResourcesDynamicResources.md): Extend the kubelet's pod resources monitoring gRPC API endpoints List and Get to include resources allocated in ResourceClaims via Dynamic Resource Allocation. (2 KB)
- [KubeletPodResourcesGet](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletPodResourcesGet.md): Enable the Get gRPC endpoint on kubelet's for Pod resources. (1 KB)
- [KubeletPodResourcesGetAllocatable](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletPodResourcesGetAllocatable.md): Enable the kubelet's pod resources GetAllocatableResources functionality. (1 KB)
- [KubeletSeparateDiskGC](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletSeparateDiskGC.md): The split image filesystem feature enables kubelet to perform garbage collection of images (read-only layers) and/or containers (writeable layers) deployed on separate filesystems. (1 KB)
- [KubeletServiceAccountTokenForCredentialProviders](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletServiceAccountTokenForCredentialProviders.md): Enable kubelet to send the service account token bound to the pod for which the image is being pulled to the credential <example-value> plugin. (1 KB)
- [KubeletTracing](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/KubeletTracing.md): Add support for distributed tracing in the kubelet. (1 KB)
- [LegacyNodeRoleBehavior](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/LegacyNodeRoleBehavior.md): When disabled, legacy behavior in service load balancers and node disruption will ignore the node-role.kubernetes.io/master label in favor of the feature-specific labels provided by… (1 KB)
- [LegacyServiceAccountTokenCleanUp](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/LegacyServiceAccountTokenCleanUp.md): Enable cleaning up Secret-based service account tokens when they are not used in a specified time (default to be one year). (1 KB)
- [LegacyServiceAccountTokenNoAutoGeneration](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/LegacyServiceAccountTokenNoAutoGeneration.md): Stop auto-generation of Secret-based service account tokens. (1 KB)
- [LegacyServiceAccountTokenTracking](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/LegacyServiceAccountTokenTracking.md): Track usage of Secret-based service account tokens. (1 KB)
- [ListFromCacheSnapshot](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ListFromCacheSnapshot.md): stages: - stage: alpha defaultValue: false fromVersion: "1.33" toVersion: "1.33" - stage: beta defaultValue: true fromVersion: "1.34" (1 KB)
- [LoadBalancerIPMode](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/LoadBalancerIPMode.md): Allows setting ipMode for Services where type is set to LoadBalancer. (1 KB)
- [LocalStorageCapacityIsolation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/LocalStorageCapacityIsolation.md): Enable the consumption of local ephemeral storage and also the sizeLimit property of an emptyDir volume. (1 KB)
- [LocalStorageCapacityIsolationFSQuotaMonitoring](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/LocalStorageCapacityIsolationFSQuotaMonitoring.md): When LocalStorageCapacityIsolation is enabled for local ephemeral storage, the backing filesystem for emptyDir volumes supports project quotas, and UserNamespacesSupport is enabled, project quotas are… (1 KB)
- [LogarithmicScaleDown](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/LogarithmicScaleDown.md): Enable semi-random selection of pods to evict on controller scaledown based on logarithmic bucketing of pod timestamps. (1 KB)
- [LoggingAlphaOptions](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/LoggingAlphaOptions.md): Allow fine-tuning of experimental, alpha-quality logging options. (1 KB)
- [LoggingBetaOptions](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/LoggingBetaOptions.md): Allow fine-tuning of experimental, beta-quality logging options. (1 KB)
- [ManifestBasedAdmissionControlConfig](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ManifestBasedAdmissionControlConfig.md): Enable loading admission webhooks and CEL-based admission policies from static manifest files on disk via the staticManifestsDir field in AdmissionConfiguration. (1 KB)
- [MatchLabelKeysInPodAffinity](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MatchLabelKeysInPodAffinity.md): Enable the matchLabelKeys and mismatchLabelKeys fields for pod (anti)affinity. (1 KB)
- [MatchLabelKeysInPodTopologySpread](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MatchLabelKeysInPodTopologySpread.md): Enable the matchLabelKeys field for Pod topology spread constraints. (1 KB)
- [MatchLabelKeysInPodTopologySpreadSelectorMerge](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MatchLabelKeysInPodTopologySpreadSelectorMerge.md): Enable merging of selectors built from matchLabelKeys into labelSelector of Pod topology spread constraints. (1 KB)
- [MaxUnavailableStatefulSet](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MaxUnavailableStatefulSet.md): Enables setting the maxUnavailable field for the rolling update strategy of a StatefulSet. (1 KB)
- [MemoryManager](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MemoryManager.md): Allows setting memory affinity for a container based on NUMA topology. (1 KB)
- [MemoryQoS](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MemoryQoS.md): Enable memory protection and usage throttling for Pods and containers using the cgroup v2 memory controller. (1 KB)
- [MinDomainsInPodTopologySpread](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MinDomainsInPodTopologySpread.md): Enable minDomains in Pod topology spread constraints. (1 KB)
- [MinimizeIPTablesRestore](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MinimizeIPTablesRestore.md): Enables new performance improvement logics in the kube-proxy iptables mode. (1 KB)
- [MixedProtocolLBService](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MixedProtocolLBService.md): Enable using different protocols in the same LoadBalancer type Service instance. (1 KB)
- [MountContainers](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MountContainers.md): Enable using utility containers on host as the volume mounter. (1 KB)
- [MountPropagation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MountPropagation.md): Enable sharing volume mounted by one container to other containers or pods. (1 KB)
- [MultiCIDRRangeAllocator](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MultiCIDRRangeAllocator.md): Enables the MultiCIDR range allocator. (1 KB)
- [MultiCIDRServiceAllocator](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MultiCIDRServiceAllocator.md): Track IP address allocations for Service cluster IPs using IPAddress objects. (1 KB)
- [MutableCSINodeAllocatableCount](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MutableCSINodeAllocatableCount.md): Make the .spec.drivers[].allocatable.count field of a CSINode mutable. (1 KB)
- [MutablePVNodeAffinity](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MutablePVNodeAffinity.md): Allow update to the .spec.nodeAffinity field of a PersistentVolume. (1 KB)
- [MutablePodResourcesForSuspendedJobs](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MutablePodResourcesForSuspendedJobs.md): Enable the ability to patch pod templates for suspended Jobs, in order to change requests or limits for infrastructure resources. (1 KB)
- [MutableSchedulingDirectivesForSuspendedJobs](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MutableSchedulingDirectivesForSuspendedJobs.md): Enable the ability to patch pod templates for suspended Jobs, in order to change the pod scheduling directives. (1 KB)
- [MutatingAdmissionPolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/MutatingAdmissionPolicy.md): Enable MutatingAdmissionPolicy support, which allows CEL mutations to be applied during admission control. (1 KB)
- [NFTablesProxyMode](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NFTablesProxyMode.md): Allow running kube-proxy in nftables mode. (1 KB)
- [NamespaceDefaultLabelName](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NamespaceDefaultLabelName.md): Configure the API Server to set an immutable label kubernetes.io/metadata.name on all namespaces, containing the namespace name. (1 KB)
- [NativeHistograms](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NativeHistograms.md): Enables Kubernetes components to expose metrics in Prometheus Native Histogram format for improved efficiency and finer bucket resolution. (1 KB)
- [NetworkPolicyEndPort](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NetworkPolicyEndPort.md): Allows you to define ports in a NetworkPolicy rule as a range of port numbers. (1 KB)
- [NetworkPolicyStatus](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NetworkPolicyStatus.md): Enable the status subresource for NetworkPolicy objects. (1 KB)
- [NewVolumeManagerReconstruction](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NewVolumeManagerReconstruction.md): Enables improved discovery of mounted volumes during kubelet startup. (1 KB)
- [NodeDeclaredFeatures](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NodeDeclaredFeatures.md): Enables Nodes to report supported features via their .status. (1 KB)
- [NodeDisruptionExclusion](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NodeDisruptionExclusion.md): Enable use of the Node label node.kubernetes.io/exclude-disruption which prevents nodes from being evacuated during zone failures. (1 KB)
- [NodeInclusionPolicyInPodTopologySpread](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NodeInclusionPolicyInPodTopologySpread.md): Enable using nodeAffinityPolicy and nodeTaintsPolicy in Pod topology spread constraints when calculating pod topology spread skew. (1 KB)
- [NodeLease](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NodeLease.md): Enable the new Lease API to report node heartbeats, which could be used as a node health signal. (1 KB)
- [NodeLifecycleConditions](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NodeLifecycleConditions.md): Enables well-known Node conditions that report drain, maintenance, and Graceful Node Shutdown lifecycle state. (1 KB)
- [NodeLogQuery](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NodeLogQuery.md): Enables querying logs of node services using the /logs endpoint. (1 KB)
- [NodeOutOfServiceVolumeDetach](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NodeOutOfServiceVolumeDetach.md): When a Node is marked out-of-service using the node.kubernetes.io/out-of-service taint, Pods on the node will be forcefully deleted if they can not tolerate this… (1 KB)
- [NodeSwap](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NodeSwap.md): Enable the kubelet to allocate swap memory for Kubernetes workloads on a node. (1 KB)
- [NominatedNodeNameForExpectation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NominatedNodeNameForExpectation.md): When enabled, kube-scheduler uses .status.nominatedNodeName to express where a Pod is going to be bound. (1 KB)
- [NonPreemptingPriority](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/NonPreemptingPriority.md): Enable preemptionPolicy field for PriorityClass and Pod. (1 KB)
- [OpenAPIEnums](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/OpenAPIEnums.md): Enables populating "enum" fields of OpenAPI schemas in the spec returned from the API server. (1 KB)
- [OpenAPIV3](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/OpenAPIV3.md): Enables the API server to publish OpenAPI v3. (1 KB)
- [OpportunisticBatching](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/OpportunisticBatching.md): Enable reusing of scheduling results from the previous scheduling cycle for equivalent pods. (1 KB)
- [OrderedNamespaceDeletion](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/OrderedNamespaceDeletion.md): While deleting namespace, the pods resources is going to be deleted before the rest of resources. (1 KB)
- [PDBUnhealthyPodEvictionPolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PDBUnhealthyPodEvictionPolicy.md): Enables the unhealthyPodEvictionPolicy field of a PodDisruptionBudget. (1 KB)
- [PVCProtection](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PVCProtection.md): Enable the prevention of a PersistentVolumeClaim (PVC) from being deleted when it is still used by any Pod. (1 KB)
- [PersistentLocalVolumes](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PersistentLocalVolumes.md): Enable the usage of local volume type in Pods. (1 KB)
- [PersistentVolumeClaimUnusedSinceTime](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PersistentVolumeClaimUnusedSinceTime.md): When enabled, the PVC protection controller adds an Unused condition to PersistentVolumeClaims that tracks whether the PVC is currently referenced by any non-terminal Pod. (1 KB)
- [PersistentVolumeLastPhaseTransitionTime](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PersistentVolumeLastPhaseTransitionTime.md): Adds a new field to PersistentVolume which holds a timestamp of when the volume last transitioned its phase. (1 KB)
- [PodAffinityNamespaceSelector](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodAffinityNamespaceSelector.md): Enable the Pod Affinity Namespace Selector and CrossNamespacePodAffinity quota scope features. (1 KB)
- [PodAndContainerStatsFromCRI](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodAndContainerStatsFromCRI.md): Configure the kubelet to gather container and pod stats from the CRI container runtime rather than gathering them from cAdvisor. (1 KB)
- [PodCertificateRequest](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodCertificateRequest.md): Enable PodCertificateRequest objects and podCertificate projected volume sources. (1 KB)
- [PodDeletionCost](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodDeletionCost.md): Enable the Pod Deletion Cost feature which allows users to influence ReplicaSet downscaling order. (1 KB)
- [PodDisruptionBudget](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodDisruptionBudget.md): Enable the PodDisruptionBudget feature. (1 KB)
- [PodDisruptionConditions](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodDisruptionConditions.md): Enabled support for appending a dedicated pod condition indicating that the pod is being deleted due to a disruption. (1 KB)
- [PodGroupPreemptionPolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodGroupPreemptionPolicy.md): Enables the support for PreemptionPolicy field in PodGroup API and Workload API. (1 KB)
- [PodHasNetworkCondition](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodHasNetworkCondition.md): Enable the kubelet to mark the PodHasNetwork condition on pods. (1 KB)
- [PodHostIPs](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodHostIPs.md): Enable the status.hostIPs field for pods and the downward API. (1 KB)
- [PodIndexLabel](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodIndexLabel.md): Enables the Job controller and StatefulSet controller to add the pod index as a label when creating new pods. (1 KB)
- [PodLevelResourceManagers](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodLevelResourceManagers.md): Enable Pod-level resource managers: the ability for the Topology, CPU, and Memory managers to use information from .spec.resources to perform NUMA alignment for an… (1 KB)
- [PodLevelResources](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodLevelResources.md): Enable Pod level resources: the ability to specify resource requests and limits at the Pod level, rather than only for specific containers. (1 KB)
- [PodLifecycleSleepAction](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodLifecycleSleepAction.md): Enables the sleep action in Container lifecycle hooks (preStop and postStart). (1 KB)
- [PodLifecycleSleepActionAllowZero](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodLifecycleSleepActionAllowZero.md): Enables setting zero value for the sleep action in container lifecycle hooks. (1 KB)
- [PodLogsQuerySplitStreams](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodLogsQuerySplitStreams.md): Enable fetching specific log streams (either stdout or stderr) from a container's log streams, using the Pod API. (1 KB)
- [PodObservedGenerationTracking](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodObservedGenerationTracking.md): Enables the kubelet to set observedGeneration in the Pod .status, and enables other components to set observedGeneration in pod conditions. (1 KB)
- [PodOverhead](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodOverhead.md): Enable the PodOverhead feature to account for pod overheads. (1 KB)
- [PodPriority](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodPriority.md): Enable the descheduling and preemption of Pods based on their priorities. (1 KB)
- [PodReadinessGates](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodReadinessGates.md): Enable the setting of PodReadinessGate field for extending Pod readiness evaluation. (1 KB)
- [PodReadyToStartContainersCondition](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodReadyToStartContainersCondition.md): Enable the kubelet to mark the PodReadyToStartContainers condition on pods. (1 KB)
- [PodSchedulingReadiness](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodSchedulingReadiness.md): Enable setting schedulingGates field to control a Pod's scheduling readiness. (1 KB)
- [PodSecurity](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodSecurity.md): Enables the PodSecurity admission plugin. (1 KB)
- [PodShareProcessNamespace](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodShareProcessNamespace.md): Enable the setting of shareProcessNamespace in a Pod for sharing a single process namespace between containers running in a pod. (1 KB)
- [PodTopologyLabelsAdmission](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodTopologyLabelsAdmission.md): Enables the PodTopologyLabels admission plugin. (1 KB)
- [PodsAPI](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PodsAPI.md): Enables the kubelet Pods API gRPC service. (1 KB)
- [PortForwardWebsockets](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PortForwardWebsockets.md): Allow WebSocket streaming of the portforward sub-protocol (port-forward) from clients requesting version v2 (v2.portforward.k8s.io) of the sub-protocol. (1 KB)
- [PreferNominatedNode](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PreferNominatedNode.md): This flag tells the scheduler whether the nominated nodes will be checked first before looping through all the other nodes in the cluster. (1 KB)
- [PreferSameTrafficDistribution](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PreferSameTrafficDistribution.md): Allows usage of the values PreferSameZone and PreferSameNode in the Service trafficDistribution field. (1 KB)
- [PreventStaticPodAPIReferences](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/PreventStaticPodAPIReferences.md): Denies Pod admission if static Pods reference other API objects. (1 KB)
- [ProbeTerminationGracePeriod](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ProbeTerminationGracePeriod.md): Enable setting probe-level terminationGracePeriodSeconds on pods. (1 KB)
- [ProcMountType](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ProcMountType.md): Enables control over the type proc mounts for containers by setting the procMount field of a Pod's securityContext. (1 KB)
- [ProxyTerminatingEndpoints](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ProxyTerminatingEndpoints.md): Enable the kube-proxy to handle terminating endpoints when ExternalTrafficPolicy=Local. (1 KB)
- [QOSReserved](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/QOSReserved.md): Allows resource reservations at the QoS level preventing pods at lower QoS levels from bursting into resources requested at higher QoS levels (memory only… (1 KB)
- [ReadOnlyAPIDataVolumes](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ReadOnlyAPIDataVolumes.md): Set configMap, secret, downwardAPI and projected volumes to be mounted read-only. (1 KB)
- [ReadWriteOncePod](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ReadWriteOncePod.md): Enables the usage of ReadWriteOncePod PersistentVolume access mode. (1 KB)
- [RecoverVolumeExpansionFailure](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RecoverVolumeExpansionFailure.md): Enables users to edit their PVCs to smaller sizes so as they can recover from previously issued volume expansion failures. (1 KB)
- [RecursiveReadOnlyMounts](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RecursiveReadOnlyMounts.md): Enables support for recursive read-only mounts. (1 KB)
- [ReduceDefaultCrashLoopBackOffDecay](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ReduceDefaultCrashLoopBackOffDecay.md): Enabled reduction of both the initial delay and the maximum delay accrued between container restarts for a node for containers in CrashLoopBackOff across the… (1 KB)
- [RelaxedDNSSearchValidation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RelaxedDNSSearchValidation.md): Relax the server side validation for the DNS search string (.spec.dnsConfig.searches) for containers. (1 KB)
- [RelaxedEnvironmentVariableValidation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RelaxedEnvironmentVariableValidation.md): Allow almost all printable ASCII characters in environment variables. (1 KB)
- [RelaxedServiceNameValidation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RelaxedServiceNameValidation.md): Enables relaxed validation for Service object names, allowing the use of RFC 1123 label names instead of RFC 1035 label names. (1 KB)
- [ReloadKubeletServerCertificateFile](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ReloadKubeletServerCertificateFile.md): Enable the kubelet TLS server to update its certificate if the specified certificate file are changed. (1 KB)
- [RemainingItemCount](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RemainingItemCount.md): Allow the API servers to show a count of remaining items in the response to a chunking list request. (1 KB)
- [RemoteRequestHeaderUID](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RemoteRequestHeaderUID.md): Enable the API server to accept UIDs (user IDs) via request header authentication. (1 KB)
- [RemoveSelfLink](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RemoveSelfLink.md): Sets the .metadata.selfLink field to blank (empty string) for all objects and collections. (1 KB)
- [RequestManagement](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RequestManagement.md): Enables managing request concurrency with prioritization and fairness at each API server. (1 KB)
- [ResilientWatchCacheInitialization](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ResilientWatchCacheInitialization.md): Enables resilient watchcache initialization to avoid controlplane overload. (1 KB)
- [ResourceHealthStatus](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ResourceHealthStatus.md): Enable the allocatedResourcesStatus field within the .status for a Pod. (1 KB)
- [ResourceLimitsPriorityFunction](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ResourceLimitsPriorityFunction.md): Enable a scheduler priority function that assigns a lowest possible score of 1 to a node that satisfies at least one of the input… (1 KB)
- [ResourceQuotaScopeSelectors](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ResourceQuotaScopeSelectors.md): Enable resource quota scope selectors. (1 KB)
- [RestartAllContainersOnContainerExits](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RestartAllContainersOnContainerExits.md): Enables the ability to specify RestartAllContainers as an action in container restartPolicyRules. (1 KB)
- [RetroactiveDefaultStorageClass](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RetroactiveDefaultStorageClass.md): Allow assigning StorageClass to unbound PVCs retroactively. (1 KB)
- [RetryGenerateName](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RetryGenerateName.md): Enables retrying of object creation when the API server is expected to generate a name. (1 KB)
- [RootCAConfigMap](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RootCAConfigMap.md): Configure the kube-controller-manager to publish a ConfigMap named kube-root-ca.crt to every namespace. (1 KB)
- [RotateKubeletClientCertificate](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RotateKubeletClientCertificate.md): Enable the rotation of the client TLS certificate on the kubelet. (1 KB)
- [RotateKubeletServerCertificate](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RotateKubeletServerCertificate.md): Enable the rotation of the server TLS certificate on the kubelet. (1 KB)
- [RunAsGroup](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RunAsGroup.md): Enable control over the primary group ID set on the init processes of containers. (1 KB)
- [RuntimeClass](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RuntimeClass.md): Enable the RuntimeClass feature for selecting container runtime configurations. (1 KB)
- [RuntimeClassInImageCriApi](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/RuntimeClassInImageCriApi.md): Enables images to be pulled based on the runtime class of the pods that reference them. (1 KB)
- [SCTPSupport](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SCTPSupport.md): Enables the SCTP protocol value in Pod, Service, Endpoints, EndpointSlice, and NetworkPolicy definitions. (1 KB)
- [SELinuxChangePolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SELinuxChangePolicy.md): Enables spec.securityContext.seLinuxChangePolicy field. (1 KB)
- [SELinuxMount](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SELinuxMount.md): Speeds up container startup by allowing kubelet to mount volumes for a Pod directly with the correct SELinux label instead of changing each file… (1 KB)
- [SELinuxMountReadWriteOncePod](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SELinuxMountReadWriteOncePod.md): Speeds up container startup by allowing kubelet to mount volumes for a Pod directly with the correct SELinux label instead of changing each file… (1 KB)
- [ScheduleDaemonSetPods](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ScheduleDaemonSetPods.md): Enable DaemonSet Pods to be scheduled by the default scheduler instead of the DaemonSet controller. (1 KB)
- [SchedulerAsyncAPICalls](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SchedulerAsyncAPICalls.md): Change the kube-scheduler to make the entire scheduling cycle free of blocking requests to the Kubernetes API server. (1 KB)
- [SchedulerAsyncPreemption](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SchedulerAsyncPreemption.md): Enable running some expensive operations within the scheduler, associated with preemption, asynchronously. (1 KB)
- [SchedulerPopFromBackoffQ](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SchedulerPopFromBackoffQ.md): Improves scheduling queue behavior by popping pods from the backoffQ when the activeQ is empty. (1 KB)
- [SchedulerQueueingHints](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SchedulerQueueingHints.md): Enables scheduler queueing hints, which benefits to reduce the useless requeuing. (1 KB)
- [SeccompDefault](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SeccompDefault.md): Enables the use of RuntimeDefault as the default seccomp profile for all workloads. (1 KB)
- [SecurityContextDeny](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SecurityContextDeny.md): This gate signals that the SecurityContextDeny admission controller is deprecated. (1 KB)
- [SelectorIndex](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SelectorIndex.md): Allows label and field based indexes in API server watch cache to accelerate list operations. (1 KB)
- [SeparateCacheWatchRPC](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SeparateCacheWatchRPC.md): Allows the API server watch cache to create a watch on a dedicated RPC. (1 KB)
- [SeparateTaintEvictionController](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SeparateTaintEvictionController.md): Enables running the taint based eviction controller, that performs Taint-based Evictions, as a standalone controller (separate from the node lifecycle controller). (1 KB)
- [ServerSideApply](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServerSideApply.md): Enables the Sever Side Apply (SSA) feature on the API Server. (1 KB)
- [ServerSideFieldValidation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServerSideFieldValidation.md): Enables server-side field validation. (1 KB)
- [ServiceAccountIssuerDiscovery](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceAccountIssuerDiscovery.md): Enable OIDC discovery endpoints (issuer and JWKS URLs) for the service account issuer in the API server. (1 KB)
- [ServiceAccountNodeAudienceRestriction](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceAccountNodeAudienceRestriction.md): This gate is used to restrict the audience for which the kubelet can request a service account token for. (1 KB)
- [ServiceAccountTokenJTI](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceAccountTokenJTI.md): Controls whether JTIs (UUIDs) are embedded into generated service account tokens, and whether these JTIs are recorded into the Kubernetes audit log for future… (1 KB)
- [ServiceAccountTokenNodeBinding](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceAccountTokenNodeBinding.md): Controls whether the API server allows binding service account tokens to Node objects. (1 KB)
- [ServiceAccountTokenNodeBindingValidation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceAccountTokenNodeBindingValidation.md): Controls whether the apiserver will validate a Node reference in service account tokens. (1 KB)
- [ServiceAccountTokenPodNodeInfo](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceAccountTokenPodNodeInfo.md): Controls whether the apiserver embeds the node name and uid for the associated node when issuing service account tokens bound to Pod objects. (1 KB)
- [ServiceAppProtocol](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceAppProtocol.md): Enables the appProtocol field on Services and Endpoints. (1 KB)
- [ServiceIPStaticSubrange](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceIPStaticSubrange.md): Enables a strategy for Services ClusterIP allocations, whereby the ClusterIP range is subdivided. (1 KB)
- [ServiceInternalTrafficPolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceInternalTrafficPolicy.md): Enables the internalTrafficPolicy field on Services (1 KB)
- [ServiceLBNodePortControl](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceLBNodePortControl.md): Enables the allocateLoadBalancerNodePorts field on Services. (1 KB)
- [ServiceLoadBalancerClass](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceLoadBalancerClass.md): Enables the loadBalancerClass field on Services. (1 KB)
- [ServiceLoadBalancerFinalizer](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceLoadBalancerFinalizer.md): Enable finalizer protection for Service load balancers. (1 KB)
- [ServiceNodeExclusion](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceNodeExclusion.md): Enable the exclusion of nodes from load balancers created by a cloud provider. (1 KB)
- [ServiceNodePortStaticSubrange](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceNodePortStaticSubrange.md): Enables the use of different port allocation strategies for NodePort Services. (1 KB)
- [ServiceTopology](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceTopology.md): Enable service to route traffic based upon the Node topology of the cluster. (1 KB)
- [ServiceTrafficDistribution](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ServiceTrafficDistribution.md): Allows usage of the optional spec.trafficDistribution field in Services. (1 KB)
- [SetHostnameAsFQDN](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SetHostnameAsFQDN.md): Enable the ability of setting Fully Qualified Domain Name(FQDN) as the hostname of a pod. (1 KB)
- [ShardedListAndWatch](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ShardedListAndWatch.md): Enable support for the shardSelector parameter on list and watch requests, allowing clients to receive a filtered subset of objects based on hash ranges… (1 KB)
