- [SidecarContainers](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SidecarContainers.md): Allow setting the restartPolicy of an init container to Always so that the container becomes a sidecar container (restartable init containers). (1 KB)
- [SizeBasedListCostEstimate](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SizeBasedListCostEstimate.md): Enables APF to use size of objects for estimating request cost. (1 KB)
- [SizeMemoryBackedVolumes](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SizeMemoryBackedVolumes.md): Enable kubelets to determine the size limit for memory-backed volumes (mainly emptyDir volumes). (1 KB)
- [SkipReadOnlyValidationGCE](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SkipReadOnlyValidationGCE.md): Skip validation that GCE PersistentDisk volumes are in read-only mode. (1 KB)
- [StableLoadBalancerNodeSet](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StableLoadBalancerNodeSet.md): Enables less load balancer re-configurations by the service controller (KCCM) as an effect of changing node state. (1 KB)
- [StaleControllerConsistencyDaemonSet](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StaleControllerConsistencyDaemonset.md): Enables behavior within the DaemonSet controller to ensure that prior writes to the API server are observed before proceeding with additional reconciliation for the… (1 KB)
- [StaleControllerConsistencyJob](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StaleControllerConsistencyJob.md): Enables behavior within the Job controller to ensure that prior writes to the API server are observed before proceeding with additional reconciliation for the… (1 KB)
- [StaleControllerConsistencyReplicaSet](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StaleControllerConsistencyReplicaSet.md): Enables behavior within the ReplicaSet controller to ensure that prior writes to the API server are observed before proceeding with additional reconciliation for the… (1 KB)
- [StaleControllerConsistencyStatefulSet](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StaleControllerConsistencyStatefulSet.md): Enables behavior within the StatefulSet controller to ensure that prior writes to the API server are observed before proceeding with additional reconciliation for the… (1 KB)
- [StartupProbe](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StartupProbe.md): Enable the startup probe in the kubelet. (1 KB)
- [StatefulSetAutoDeletePVC](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StatefulSetAutoDeletePVC.md): Allows the use of the optional .spec.persistentVolumeClaimRetentionPolicy field, providing control over the deletion of PVCs in a StatefulSet's lifecycle. (1 KB)
- [StatefulSetMinReadySeconds](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StatefulSetMinReadySeconds.md): Allows minReadySeconds to be respected by the StatefulSet controller. (1 KB)
- [StatefulSetRecreateStrategy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StatefulSetRecreateStrategy.md): Enables the Recreate update strategy for StatefulSets, which deletes all of a StatefulSet's Pods before creating new Pods that reflect modifications made to the… (1 KB)
- [StatefulSetStartOrdinal](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StatefulSetStartOrdinal.md): Allow configuration of the start ordinal in a StatefulSet. (1 KB)
- [StorageCapacityScoring](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StorageCapacityScoring.md): The feature gate VolumeCapacityPriority was used in v1.32 to support storage that are statically provisioned. (1 KB)
- [StorageNamespaceIndex](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StorageNamespaceIndex.md): Enables a namespace indexer for namespace scoped resources in API server cache to accelerate list operations. (1 KB)
- [StorageObjectInUseProtection](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StorageObjectInUseProtection.md): Postpone the deletion of PersistentVolume or PersistentVolumeClaim objects if they are still being used. (1 KB)
- [StorageVersionAPI](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StorageVersionAPI.md): Enable the storage version API. (1 KB)
- [StorageVersionHash](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StorageVersionHash.md): Allow API servers to expose the storage version hash in the discovery. (1 KB)
- [StorageVersionMigrator](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StorageVersionMigrator.md): Enables the migration of the storage version of a resource. (1 KB)
- [StreamingCollectionEncodingToJSON](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StreamingCollectionEncodingToJSON.md): Allow the API server JSON encoder to encode collections item by item, instead of all at once. (1 KB)
- [StreamingCollectionEncodingToProtobuf](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StreamingCollectionEncodingToProtobuf.md): Allow the API server Protobuf encoder to encode collections item by item, instead of all at once. (1 KB)
- [StreamingProxyRedirects](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StreamingProxyRedirects.md): Instructs the API server to intercept (and follow) redirects from the backend (kubelet) for streaming requests. (1 KB)
- [StrictCostEnforcementForVAP](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StrictCostEnforcementForVAP.md): Apply strict CEL cost validation for ValidatingAdmissionPolicies. (1 KB)
- [StrictCostEnforcementForWebhooks](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StrictCostEnforcementForWebhooks.md): Apply strict CEL cost validation for matchConditions within admission webhooks. (1 KB)
- [StrictIPCIDRValidation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StrictIPCIDRValidation.md): Use stricter validation for fields containing IP addresses and CIDR values. (1 KB)
- [StructuredAuthenticationConfiguration](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StructuredAuthenticationConfiguration.md): Enable structured authentication configuration for the API server. (1 KB)
- [StructuredAuthenticationConfigurationEgressSelector](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StructuredAuthenticationConfigurationEgressSelector.md): Enables Egress Selector in Structured Authentication Configuration. (1 KB)
- [StructuredAuthenticationConfigurationJWKSMetrics](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StructuredAuthenticationConfigurationJWKSMetrics.md): Enables additional metrics for JSON Web Key Set (JWKS) operations in JWT authenticators configured via --authentication-config. (1 KB)
- [StructuredAuthorizationConfiguration](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/StructuredAuthorizationConfiguration.md): Enable structured authorization configuration, so that cluster administrators can specify more than one authorization webhook in the API server handler chain. (1 KB)
- [SupplementalGroupsPolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SupplementalGroupsPolicy.md): Enables support for fine-grained SupplementalGroups control. (1 KB)
- [SupportIPVSProxyMode](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SupportIPVSProxyMode.md): Enable providing in-cluster service load balancing using IPVS. (1 KB)
- [SupportNodePidsLimit](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SupportNodePidsLimit.md): Enable the support to limiting PIDs on the Node. (1 KB)
- [SupportPodPidsLimit](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SupportPodPidsLimit.md): Enable the support to limiting PIDs in Pods. (1 KB)
- [SuspendJob](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SuspendJob.md): Enable support to suspend and resume Jobs. (1 KB)
- [Sysctls](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/Sysctls.md): Enable support for namespaced kernel parameters (sysctls) that can be set for each pod. (1 KB)
- [SystemdWatchdog](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/SystemdWatchdog.md): Allow using systemd watchdog to monitor the health status of kubelet. (1 KB)
- [TTLAfterFinished](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TTLAfterFinished.md): Allow a TTL controller to clean up resources after they finish execution. (1 KB)
- [TaintBasedEvictions](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TaintBasedEvictions.md): Enable evicting pods from nodes based on taints on Nodes and tolerations on Pods. (1 KB)
- [TaintNodesByCondition](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TaintNodesByCondition.md): Enable automatic tainting nodes based on node conditions. (1 KB)
- [TaintTolerationComparisonOperators](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TaintTolerationComparisonOperators.md): Enables numeric comparison operators (Lt and Gt) for tolerations. (1 KB)
- [TokenRequest](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TokenRequest.md): Enable the TokenRequest endpoint on service account resources. (1 KB)
- [TokenRequestProjection](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TokenRequestProjection.md): Enable the injection of service account tokens into a Pod through a projected volume. (1 KB)
- [TokenRequestServiceAccountUIDValidation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TokenRequestServiceAccountUIDValidation.md): This is used to ensure that the UID provided in the TokenRequest matches the UID of the ServiceAccount for which the token is being… (1 KB)
- [TopologyAwareHints](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TopologyAwareHints.md): Enables topology aware routing based on topology hints in EndpointSlices. (1 KB)
- [TopologyAwareWorkloadScheduling](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TopologyAwareWorkloadScheduling.md): Enable topology-aware scheduling for Workloads. (1 KB)
- [TopologyManager](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TopologyManager.md): Enable a mechanism to coordinate fine-grained hardware resource assignments for different components in Kubernetes. (1 KB)
- [TopologyManagerPolicyAlphaOptions](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TopologyManagerPolicyAlphaOptions.md): Allow fine-tuning of topology manager policies, experimental, Alpha-quality options. (1 KB)
- [TopologyManagerPolicyBetaOptions](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TopologyManagerPolicyBetaOptions.md): Allow fine-tuning of topology manager policies, experimental, Beta-quality options. (1 KB)
- [TopologyManagerPolicyOptions](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TopologyManagerPolicyOptions.md): Enable fine-tuning of topology manager policies. (1 KB)
- [TranslateStreamCloseWebsocketRequests](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/TranslateStreamCloseWebsocketRequests.md): Allow WebSocket streaming of the remote command sub-protocol (exec, cp, attach) from clients requesting version 5 (v5) of the sub-protocol. (1 KB)
- [UnauthenticatedHTTP2DOSMitigation](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/UnauthenticatedHTTP2DOSMitigation.md): Enables HTTP/2 Denial of Service (DoS) mitigations for unauthenticated clients. (1 KB)
- [UnknownVersionInteroperabilityProxy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/UnknownVersionInteroperabilityProxy.md): Proxy resource requests to the correct peer kube-apiserver when multiple kube-apiservers exist at varied versions. (1 KB)
- [UnlockWhileProcessingFIFO](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/UnlockWhileProcessingFIFO.md): Enable use of a FIFO queue within client-go that unlocks while processing events. (1 KB)
- [UserNamespacesHostNetworkSupport](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/UserNamespacesHostNetworkSupport.md): When enabled, pods are allowed to use both hostNetwork and User Namespaces simultaneously. (1 KB)
- [UserNamespacesPodSecurityStandards](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/UserNamespacesPodSecurityStandards.md): Enable Pod Security Standards policies relaxation for pods that run with namespaces. (1 KB)
- [UserNamespacesStatelessPodsSupport](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/UserNamespacesStatelessPodsSupport.md): Enable user namespace support for stateless Pods. (1 KB)
- [UserNamespacesSupport](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/UserNamespacesSupport.md): Enable user namespace support for Pods. (1 KB)
- [ValidateProxyRedirects](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ValidateProxyRedirects.md): This flag controls whether the API server should validate that redirects are only followed to the same host. (1 KB)
- [ValidatingAdmissionPolicy](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ValidatingAdmissionPolicy.md): Enable ValidatingAdmissionPolicy support for CEL validations be used in Admission Control. (1 KB)
- [VolumeAttributesClass](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/VolumeAttributesClass.md): Enable support for VolumeAttributesClasses. (1 KB)
- [VolumeBindMountOptions](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/VolumeBindMountOptions.md): Enables setting bind mount options (noexec, nodev, nosuid) per container volume mount using the bindMountOptions field in volumeMounts. (1 KB)
- [VolumeCapacityPriority](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/VolumeCapacityPriority.md): Enable support for prioritizing nodes in different topologies based on available PV capacity. (1 KB)
- [VolumeLimitScaling](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/VolumeLimitScaling.md): Enables volume limit scaling for CSI drivers. (1 KB)
- [VolumePVCDataSource](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/VolumePVCDataSource.md): Enable support for specifying an existing PVC as a DataSource. (1 KB)
- [VolumeScheduling](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/VolumeScheduling.md): Enable volume topology aware scheduling and make the PersistentVolumeClaim (PVC) binding aware of scheduling decisions. (1 KB)
- [VolumeSnapshotDataSource](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/VolumeSnapshotDataSource.md): Enable volume snapshot data source support. (1 KB)
- [VolumeSubpath](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/VolumeSubpath.md): Allow mounting a subpath of a volume in a container. (1 KB)
- [VolumeSubpathEnvExpansion](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/VolumeSubpathEnvExpansion.md): Enable subPathExpr field for expanding environment variables into a subPath. (1 KB)
- [WarningHeaders](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WarningHeaders.md): Allow sending warning headers in API responses. (1 KB)
- [WatchBookmark](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WatchBookmark.md): Enable support for watch bookmark events. (1 KB)
- [WatchCacheInitializationPostStartHook](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WatchCacheInitializationPostStartHook.md): Enables post-start-hook for watchcache initialization to be part of readyz (with timeout). (1 KB)
- [WatchFromStorageWithoutResourceVersion](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WatchFromStorageWithoutResourceVersion.md): Enables watches without resourceVersion to be served from storage. (1 KB)
- [WatchList](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WatchList.md): Enable support for streaming initial state of objects in watch requests. (1 KB)
- [WatchListClient](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WatchListClient.md): Allows an API client to request a stream of data rather than fetching a full list. (1 KB)
- [WatchListCompression](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WatchListCompression.md): Enables compression for watch list responses from the API server. (1 KB)
- [WinDSR](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WinDSR.md): Allows kube-proxy to create DSR loadbalancers for Windows. (1 KB)
- [WinOverlay](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WinOverlay.md): Allows kube-proxy to run in overlay mode for Windows. (1 KB)
- [WindowsCPUAndMemoryAffinity](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WindowsCPUAndMemoryAffinity.md): Add CPU and Memory Affinity support to Windows nodes with CPUManager, MemoryManager and topology manager. (1 KB)
- [WindowsEndpointSliceProxying](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WindowsEndpointSliceProxying.md): When enabled, kube-proxy running on Windows will use EndpointSlices as the primary data source instead of Endpoints, enabling scalability and performance improvements. (1 KB)
- [WindowsGMSA](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WindowsGMSA.md): Enables passing of GMSA credential specs from pods to container runtimes. (1 KB)
- [WindowsGracefulNodeShutdown](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WindowsGracefulNodeShutdown.md): Enables support for windows node graceful shutdown in kubelet. (1 KB)
- [WindowsHostNetwork](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WindowsHostNetwork.md): Enables support for joining Windows containers to a hosts' network namespace. (1 KB)
- [WindowsHostProcessContainers](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WindowsHostProcessContainers.md): Enables support for Windows HostProcess containers. (1 KB)
- [WindowsRunAsUserName](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WindowsRunAsUserName.md): Enable support for running applications in Windows containers with as a non-default user. (1 KB)
- [WorkloadAwarePreemption](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WorkloadAwarePreemption.md): Enables the support for Workload-aware preemption. (1 KB)
- [WorkloadWithJob](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/WorkloadWithJob.md): Enables the Job controller to compile a Job's .spec.scheduling configuration into Workload and PodGroup objects before it creates any Pods. (1 KB)
- [ZeroLimitedNominalConcurrencyShares](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/ZeroLimitedNominalConcurrencyShares.md): Allow priority & fairness in the API server to use a zero value for the nominalConcurrencyShares field of the limited section of a priority… (1 KB)
- [ExtendWebSocketsToKubelet](library/kubernetes/reference/command-line-tools-reference/feature-gates/extend-websockets-to-kubelet.md): When ExtendWebSocketsToKubelet is enabled and a kubelet node advertises support, exec/attach/portforward streams are proxied directly to the kubelet rather than being translated or tunneled… (1 KB)

- [Add-ons](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/addons.md): Resources that extend the functionality of Kubernetes. (1 KB)
- [Admission Controller](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/admission-controller.md): A piece of code that intercepts requests to the Kubernetes API server prior to persistence of the object. (1 KB)
- [Affinity](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/affinity.md): In Kubernetes, affinity is a set of rules that give hints to the scheduler about where to place pods. (1 KB)
- [Aggregation Layer](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/aggregation-layer.md): The aggregation layer lets you install additional Kubernetes-style APIs in your cluster. (1 KB)
- [Annotation](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/annotation.md): A key-value pair that is used to attach arbitrary non-identifying metadata to objects. (1 KB)
- [API-initiated eviction](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/api-eviction.md): API-initiated eviction is the process by which you use the Eviction API to create an Eviction object that triggers graceful pod termination. (1 KB)
- [API Group](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/api-group.md): A set of related paths in Kubernetes API. (1 KB)
- [API resource](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/api-resource.md): An entity in the Kubernetes type system, corresponding to an endpoint on the Kubernetes API. (1 KB)
- [App Container](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/app-container.md): Application containers (or app containers) are the containers in a pod that are started after any init containers have completed. (1 KB)
- [Application Architect](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/application-architect.md): A person responsible for the high-level design of an application. (1 KB)
- [Application Developer](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/application-developer.md): A person who writes an application that runs in a Kubernetes cluster. (1 KB)
- [Applications](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/applications.md): The layer where various containerized applications run. (1 KB)
- [Approver](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/approver.md): A person who can review and approve Kubernetes code contributions. (1 KB)
- [cAdvisor](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cadvisor.md): cAdvisor (Container Advisor) provides container users an understanding of the resource usage and performance characteristics of their running containers. (1 KB)
- [Container Device Interface (CDI)](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cdi.md): The Container Device Interface (CDI) is a specification for how to configure devices inside containers. (1 KB)
- [Common Expression Language](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cel.md): A general-purpose expression language that's designed to be fast, portable, and safe to execute. (1 KB)
- [Certificate](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/certificate.md): A cryptographically secure file used to validate access to the Kubernetes cluster. (1 KB)
- [cgroup (control group)](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cgroup.md): A group of Linux processes with optional resource isolation, accounting and limits. (1 KB)
- [CIDR](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cidr.md): CIDR (Classless Inter-Domain Routing) is a notation for describing blocks of IP addresses and is used heavily in various networking configurations. (1 KB)
- [CLA (Contributor License Agreement)](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cla.md): Terms under which a contributor grants a license to an open source project for their contributions. (1 KB)
- [Cloud Controller Manager](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cloud-controller-manager.md): A Kubernetes control plane component that embeds cloud-specific control logic. (1 KB)
- [Cloud Provider](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cloud-provider.md): A business or other organization that offers a cloud computing platform. (1 KB)
- [Cluster Architect](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cluster-architect.md): A person who designs infrastructure that involves one or more Kubernetes clusters. (1 KB)
- [Cluster Infrastructure](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cluster-infrastructure.md): The infrastructure layer provides and maintains VMs, networking, security groups and others. (1 KB)
- [Cluster Operations](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cluster-operations.md): The work involved in managing a Kubernetes cluster: managing day-to-day operations, and co-ordinating upgrades. (1 KB)
- [Cluster Operator](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cluster-operator.md): A person who configures, controls, and monitors clusters. (1 KB)
- [Cluster](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cluster.md): A set of worker machines, called nodes, that run containerized applications. (1 KB)
- [Cloud Native Computing Foundation (CNCF)](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cncf.md): The Cloud Native Computing Foundation (CNCF) builds sustainable ecosystems and fosters a community around projects that orchestrate containers as part of a microservices architecture. (1 KB)
- [Container network interface (CNI)](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cni.md): Container network interface (CNI) plugins are a type of Network plugin that adheres to the appc/CNI specification. (1 KB)
- [Code Contributor](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/code-contributor.md): A person who develops and contributes code to the Kubernetes open source codebase. (1 KB)
- [Condition](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/condition.md): A condition is a field in a Kubernetes resource's status that describes the current state of that resource. (1 KB)
- [ConfigMap](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/configmap.md): An API object used to store non-confidential data in key-value pairs. (1 KB)
- [Container Environment Variables](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/container-env-variables.md): Container environment variables are name=value pairs that provide useful information into containers running in a pod (1 KB)
- [Container Lifecycle Hooks](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/container-lifecycle-hooks.md): The lifecycle hooks expose events in the Container management lifecycle and let the user run code when the events occur. (1 KB)
- [Container Runtime](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/container-runtime.md): A fundamental component that empowers Kubernetes to run containers effectively. (1 KB)
- [Container](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/container.md): A lightweight and portable executable image that contains software and all of its dependencies. (1 KB)
- [containerd](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/containerd.md): A container runtime with an emphasis on simplicity, robustness and portability (1 KB)
- [Contributor](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/contributor.md): Someone who donates code, documentation, or their time to help the Kubernetes project or community. (1 KB)
- [Control Plane](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/control-plane.md): The container orchestration layer that exposes the API and interfaces to define, deploy, and manage the lifecycle of containers. (1 KB)
- [Controller](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/controller.md): In Kubernetes, controllers are control loops that watch the state of your cluster, then make or request changes where needed. (1 KB)
- [CRI-O](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cri-o.md): A tool that lets you use OCI container runtimes with Kubernetes CRI. (1 KB)
- [Container Runtime Interface (CRI)](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cri.md): The main protocol for the communication between the kubelet and Container Runtime. (1 KB)
- [CronJob](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/cronjob.md): Manages a Job that runs on a periodic schedule. (1 KB)
- [Container Storage Interface (CSI)](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/csi.md): The Container Storage Interface (CSI) defines a standard interface to expose storage systems to containers. (1 KB)
- [CustomResourceDefinition](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/customresourcedefinition.md): A kind of API object that defines a new custom API to add to your Kubernetes API server, without building a complete custom server. (1 KB)
- [DaemonSet](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/daemonset.md): Ensures a copy of a Pod is running across a set of nodes in a cluster. (1 KB)
- [Data Plane](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/data-plane.md): The layer that provides capacity such as CPU, memory, network, and storage so that the containers can run and connect to a network. (1 KB)
- [Deployment](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/deployment.md): An API object that manages a replicated application, typically by running Pods with no local state. (1 KB)

- [Disruption](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/disruption.md): Disruptions are events that lead to one or more Pods going out of service. (1 KB)

- [Drain](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/drain.md): The process of safely evicting Pods from a Node to prepare it for maintenance or removal from a cluster. (1 KB)
- [Duration](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/duration.md): A string value representing an amount of time. (1 KB)
- [Dynamic Volume Provisioning](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/dynamic-volume-provisioning.md): Allows users to request automatic creation of storage Volumes. (1 KB)
- [EndpointSlice](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/endpoint-slice.md): EndpointSlices track the IP addresses of backend endpoints. (1 KB)

- [etcd](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/etcd.md): Consistent and highly-available key value store used as Kubernetes' backing store for all cluster data. (1 KB)
- [Event](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/event.md): A Kubernetes object that describes state changes or notable occurrences in the cluster. (1 KB)
- [Eviction](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/eviction.md): Eviction is the process of terminating one or more Pods on Nodes. (1 KB)
- [Extensions](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/extensions.md): Extensions are software components that extend and deeply integrate with Kubernetes to support new types of hardware. (1 KB)
- [Feature gate](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/feature-gates.md): Feature gates are a set of keys (opaque string values) that you can use to control which Kubernetes features are enabled in your cluster. (1 KB)

- [FlexVolume](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/flexvolume.md): FlexVolume is a deprecated interface for creating out-of-tree volume plugins. (1 KB)
- [Garbage Collection](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/garbage-collection.md): Garbage collection is a collective term for the various mechanisms Kubernetes uses to clean up cluster resources. (1 KB)

- [Group Version Resource](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/group-version-resource.md): Means of representing specific Kubernetes APIs uniquely. (1 KB)
- [Helm Chart](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/helm-chart.md): A package of pre-configured Kubernetes configurations that can be managed with the Helm tool. (1 KB)
- [Horizontal Pod Autoscaler](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/horizontal-pod-autoscaler.md): An object that automatically scales the number of pod replicas, based on targeted resource utilization or custom metric targets. (1 KB)
- [HostAliases](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/host-aliases.md): A HostAliases is a mapping between the IP address and hostname to be injected into a Pod's hosts file. (1 KB)
- [Image](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/image.md): Stored instance of a container that holds a set of software needed to run an application. (1 KB)
- [Immutable Infrastructure](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/immutable-infrastructure.md): Immutable Infrastructure refers to computer infrastructure (virtual machines, containers, network appliances) that cannot be changed once deployed. (1 KB)

- [Kubelet](library/kubernetes/reference/glossary/kubelet.md): An agent that runs on each node in the cluster. (1 KB)
